// File: src/main/java/guru/springframework/spring7restmvc/entities/Beer.java (java)
package guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.entities;

import guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.model.BeerStyle;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;
import lombok.*;
import org.hibernate.annotations.CreationTimestamp;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.annotations.UuidGenerator;
import org.hibernate.annotations.UpdateTimestamp;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Created by jt, Spring Framework Guru.
 */
@Getter
@Setter
@Builder
@Entity
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
public class Beer {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(generator = "UUID")
    @UuidGenerator
    @Column(length = 36, columnDefinition = "varchar", updatable = false, nullable = false)
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.CHAR)
    private UUID id;

    @Version
    private Integer version;

    @NotNull
    @NotBlank
    @Size(max = 50)
    @Column(length = 50)
    private String beerName;

    @NotNull
    @JdbcTypeCode(value = SqlTypes.SMALLINT)
    private BeerStyle beerStyle;

    @NotNull
    @NotBlank
    @Size(max = 255)
    private String upc;
    private Integer quantityOnHand;

    @NotNull
    private BigDecimal price;

    @OneToMany(mappedBy = "beer")
    private Set<BeerOrderLine> beerOrderLines;

    @ManyToMany
    @JoinTable(name = "beer_category",
            joinColumns = @JoinColumn(name = "beer_id"),
       inverseJoinColumns = @JoinColumn(name = "category_id"))
    private Set<Category> categories;

    @CreationTimestamp
    private LocalDateTime createdDate;

    @UpdateTimestamp
    private LocalDateTime updateDate;
}

package guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.entities;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;
import org.hibernate.annotations.*;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

@Getter
@Setter
@Entity
@NoArgsConstructor
@Builder
public class BeerOrder {

    public BeerOrder(UUID id, Long version, Timestamp createdDate, Timestamp lastModifiedDate, String customerRef, Customer customer, Set<BeerOrderLine> beerOrderLines) {
        this.id = id;
        this.version = version;
        this.createdDate = createdDate;
        this.lastModifiedDate = lastModifiedDate;
        this.customerRef = customerRef;
        this.setCustomer(customer);
        this.beerOrderLines = beerOrderLines;
    }

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(generator = "UUID")
    @UuidGenerator
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.CHAR)
    @Column(length = 36, columnDefinition = "varchar(36)", updatable = false, nullable = false )
    private UUID id;

    @Version
    private Long version;

    @CreationTimestamp
    @Column(updatable = false)
    private Timestamp createdDate;

    @UpdateTimestamp
    private Timestamp lastModifiedDate;

    public boolean isNew() {
        return this.id == null;
    }

    private String customerRef;

    @ManyToOne
    private Customer customer;

    public void setCustomer(Customer customer) {
        this.customer = customer;
        customer.getBeerOrders().add(this);
    }


    @OneToMany(mappedBy = "beerOrder")
    private Set<BeerOrderLine> beerOrderLines;




}

// File: src/main/java/guru/springframework/spring7restmvc/entities/Category.java (java)
package guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.entities;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.CreationTimestamp;
import org.hibernate.annotations.GenericGenerator;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.annotations.UpdateTimestamp;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

@Getter
@Setter
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Entity
public class Category {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(generator = "UUID")
    @GenericGenerator(name = "UUID", strategy = "org.hibernate.id.UUIDGenerator")
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.CHAR)
    @Column(length = 36, columnDefinition = "varchar(36)", updatable = false, nullable = false )
    private UUID id;

    @Version
    private Long version;

    @CreationTimestamp
    @Column(updatable = false)
    private Timestamp createdDate;

    @UpdateTimestamp
    private Timestamp lastModifiedDate;

    private String description;

    @ManyToMany
    @JoinTable(name = "beer_category",
      joinColumns = @JoinColumn(name = "category_id"),
      inverseJoinColumns = @JoinColumn(name = "beer_id"))
    private Set<Beer> beers;



    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (this == o) return true;
        if (!(o instanceof Category)) return false;
        if (!super.equals(o)) return false;

        Category category = (Category) o;

        return getDescription() != null ? getDescription().equals(category.getDescription()) : category.getDescription() == null;
    }

    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        int result = super.hashCode();
        result = 31 * result + (getDescription() != null ? getDescription().hashCode() : 0);
        return result;
    }
}

// File: src/main/java/guru/springframework/spring7restmvc/controller/BeerController.java (java)
package guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.controller;

import guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.model.BeerDTO;
import guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.model.BeerStyle;
import guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.services.BeerService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.validation.annotation.Validated;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Created by jt, Spring Framework Guru.
 */
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@RestController
public class BeerController {

    public static final String BEER_PATH = "/api/v1/beer";
    public static final String BEER_PATH_ID = BEER_PATH + "/{beerId}";

    private final BeerService beerService;

    @PatchMapping(BEER_PATH_ID)
    public ResponseEntity updateBeerPatchById(@PathVariable("beerId")UUID beerId, @RequestBody BeerDTO beer){

        beerService.patchBeerById(beerId, beer);

        return new ResponseEntity(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT);
    }

    @DeleteMapping(BEER_PATH_ID)
    public ResponseEntity deleteById(@PathVariable("beerId") UUID beerId){

        if(! beerService.deleteById(beerId)){
            throw new NotFoundException();
        }

        return new ResponseEntity(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT);
    }

    @PutMapping(BEER_PATH_ID)
    public ResponseEntity updateById(@PathVariable("beerId")UUID beerId, @Validated @RequestBody BeerDTO beer){

        if( beerService.updateBeerById(beerId, beer).isEmpty()){
            throw new NotFoundException();
        }

        return new ResponseEntity(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT);
    }

    @PostMapping(BEER_PATH)
    public ResponseEntity handlePost(@Validated @RequestBody BeerDTO beer){

        BeerDTO savedBeer = beerService.saveNewBeer(beer);

        HttpHeaders headers = new HttpHeaders();
        headers.add("Location", BEER_PATH + "/" + savedBeer.getId().toString());

        return new ResponseEntity(headers, HttpStatus.CREATED);
    }

    @GetMapping(value = BEER_PATH)
    public Page<BeerDTO> listBeers(@RequestParam(required = false) String beerName,
                                   @RequestParam(required = false) BeerStyle beerStyle,
                                   @RequestParam(required = false) Boolean showInventory,
                                   @RequestParam(required = false) Integer pageNumber,
                                   @RequestParam(required = false) Integer pageSize){
        return beerService.listBeers(beerName, beerStyle, showInventory, pageNumber, pageSize);
    }


    @GetMapping(value = BEER_PATH_ID)
    public BeerDTO getBeerById(@PathVariable("beerId") UUID beerId){

        log.debug("Get Beer by Id - in controller");

        return beerService.getBeerById(beerId).orElseThrow(NotFoundException::new);
    }

}

package guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.bootstrap;

import guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.repositories.BeerRepository;
import guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.repositories.CustomerRepository;
import guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.services.BeerCsvService;
import guru.springframework.spring7restmvc.services.BeerCsvServiceImpl;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.data.jpa.test.autoconfigure.DataJpaTest;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

@DataJpaTest
@Import(BeerCsvServiceImpl.class)
class BootstrapDataTest {

    @Autowired
    BeerRepository beerRepository;

    @Autowired
    CustomerRepository customerRepository;

    @Autowired
    BeerCsvService csvService;

    BootstrapData bootstrapData;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        bootstrapData = new BootstrapData(beerRepository, customerRepository, csvService);
    }

    @Test
    void Testrun() throws Exception {
        bootstrapData.run(null);

        assertThat(beerRepository.count()).isEqualTo(2413);
        assertThat(customerRepository.count()).isEqualTo(3);
    }
}

- BeerOrder 1—* BeerOrderLine (line owns via @ManyToOne beerOrder; order inverse)
- Beer 1—* BeerOrderLine (line owns via @ManyToOne beer; beer inverse)
- Beer *—* Category (both sides @ManyToMany @JoinTable beer_category, both owners)

Type inconsistencies:
- Beer/Customer: Integer version, LocalDateTime timestamps
- BeerOrder/BeerOrderLine/Category: Long version, java.sql.Timestamp
- Category uses @GenericGenerator (deprecated) instead of @UuidGenerator
- Category has no @Builder; others do
- BeerOrder has custom constructor + setCustomer sync helper

Paging/sorting:
- buildPageRequest: 1-based→0-based, default page 0, size 25, clamp 1000, sort by beerName asc
- Page<BeerDTO> returned by controller → JSON has content/pageable/totalElements/...
- showInventory=false nulls quantityOnHand on detached entities
- Known edge: pageSize=0/negative → IllegalArgumentException → 500

Error handling:
- NotFoundException @ResponseStatus 404
- CustomErrorController @ControllerAdvice: TransactionSystemException→400, MethodArgumentNotValidException→400 with field error list

Validation:
- DTO @NotBlank/@NotNull, Beer entity @Size/@NotNull
- @Validated on controller post/put

Profiles:
- default: H2 in-memory, flyway disabled, docker compose disabled
- localmysql: MariaDB, flyway enabled, ddl-auto=validate, Hikari pool, SQL logging
- My docker.compose.enabled=false local change (documented)

Tests:
- DataJpaTest: BootstrapDataTest (expects 2413 beers, 3 customers), CustomerRepositoryTest, BeerRepositoryTest
- SpringBootTest: BeerControllerIT (16 tests), CustomerControllerIT, Spring7RestMvcApplicationTests, BeerOrderRepositoryTest
- WebMvcTest: BeerControllerTest, CustomerControllerTest (Mockito — fail in this env)
- MySqlIT: Testcontainers MySQL 9.2, @ActiveProfiles localmysql — needs Docker (won't run here)
- BeerCsvServiceImplTest: plain unit test

Gaps/risks:
- No order/category service, DTO, mapper, controller → not exposed
- Category.equals/hashCode on mutable description
- BeerOrder.setCustomer NPE if null; builder always requires non-null customer
- Both-sided M2M owners (no mappedBy)
- V4 drop order wrong
- Uninitialized Sets (Beer.categories, BeerOrder.beerOrderLines, Beer.beerOrderLines, BeerOrderLine none) → null collections
- No cascade/orphanRemoval → order lines must be persisted explicitly
- Eager @ManyToOne on lines → N+1 risk
- No @JoinColumn on @ManyToOne → implicit naming
- Column length mismatches not validated
- version type inconsistency (integer vs bigint) — note DB columns: beer.version integer, category/beer_order bigint

Let me be careful and accurate on a few points:

- `Beer.categories` has no initialization → null. `Category.beers` → null. `BeerOrder.beerOrderLines` → null. `Beer.beerOrderLines` → null. `Customer.beerOrders` → new HashSet<>().

- For the M2M with both sides owning: Hibernate actually may issue problems on persist (duplicate insert into join table). When both sides manage the same join table, persisting both directions can cause duplicate key errors. This is a real risk. Let me note it as a risk to be validated in branch 106.

- `Category` `@AllArgsConstructor` includes `Set<Beer> beers`. And `@NoArgsConstructor`.

- BeerOrder's explicit constructor is used by @Builder. Since @Builder on class + explicit constructor: Lombok docs say if a constructor exists, @Builder uses it (must be all-args). Yes.

- `isNew()` in BeerOrder/BeerOrderLine — unused currently.

Here's the complete deep dive on the codebase as it stands at local-105.

What it is / stack

A Spring Boot 4 / Spring Framework 7 REST app (the "Spring 7 Rest MVC" course project). Concrete versions: Spring Boot 4.0.6, Spring 7.0.7, Java 25, Hibernate ORM 7.2.12, Tomcat 11.0.21, MySQL connector 9.7, H2 2.4.240, Flyway 11.14.1, MapStruct 1.6.3, Lombok, OpenCSV 5.12. Build is Maven, with the compiler plugin wiring MapStruct + Lombok as annotation processors (-Amapstruct.defaultComponentModel=spring) and a failsafe plugin for *IT integration tests.

Layered architecture and request flow

HTTP → Controller → Service (interface) → Repository → JPA/Hibernate → DB
                       ↕ DTO⇄Entity (MapStruct)
  • Controllers speak DTOs, repositories speak entities, and MapStruct maps between them.
  • Services are split into an interface plus two implementations: an in-memory one and a JPA one marked @Primary (so JPA wins by default). The in-memory versions exist mainly as test doubles / teaching contrasts.

Domain model (entities) and current relationships

Five entities now exist:

  • Beer — @Builder, UUID id (@UuidGenerator + @JdbcTypeCode(CHAR)), Integer version (@Version), beerName (@Size 50), beerStyle (SMALLINT-mapped enum), upc, quantityOnHand, price, LocalDateTime created/update timestamps.
  • Customer — UUID id, name, email, Integer version, LocalDateTime timestamps, and @Builder.Default Set<BeerOrder> beerOrders = new HashSet<>().
  • BeerOrder — UUID id, Long version, java.sql.Timestamp timestamps, customerRef, @ManyToOne Customer customer, @OneToMany(mappedBy="beerOrder") Set<BeerOrderLine> beerOrderLines, an isNew(), plus a hand-written all-args constructor and a setCustomer sync helper.
  • BeerOrderLine — UUID id, Long version, Timestamp timestamps, Integer orderQuantity = 0, quantityAllocated = 0, @ManyToOne BeerOrder beerOrder, @ManyToOne Beer beer.
  • Category — UUID id (via deprecated @GenericGenerator), Long version, Timestamp timestamps, description, @ManyToMany Set<Beer> beers, and equals/hashCode on description.

The relationship graph:

| Association | Owning side | Inverse side | Column/table |
|---|---|---|---|
| Customer ↔ BeerOrder | BeerOrder.customer @ManyToOne | Customer.beerOrders (mappedBy) | beer_order.customer_id |
| BeerOrder ↔ BeerOrderLine | BeerOrderLine.beerOrder | BeerOrder.beerOrderLines | beer_order_line.beer_order_id |
| Beer ↔ BeerOrderLine | BeerOrderLine.beer | Beer.beerOrderLines | beer_order_line.beer_id |
| Beer ↔ Category | both sides @ManyToMany @JoinTable | — (no mappedBy) | beer_category |

Persistence layer

  • Repositories: BeerRepository (derived queries findAllByBeerNameIsLikeIgnoreCase, findAllByBeerStyle, and the combined one — all taking Pageable, returning Page<Beer>), CustomerRepository (plain), BeerOrderRepository (plain). No CategoryRepository.
  • Schema is owned by Flyway (ddl-auto=validate): V1 creates beer/customer, V2 adds customer.email, V3 adds the order tables, V4 adds category + the beer_category join table. All are applied and recorded (history v1–v4).
  • UUIDs persist as varchar(36) via @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.CHAR); the enum beerStyle persists as smallint.

Service layer logic

BeerServiceJPA.listBeers(...) is the most interesting piece:

  • buildPageRequest(pageNumber, pageSize) implements three policies: 1-based→0-based (pageNumber - 1), a default page size of 25, a hard ceiling of 1000, and a Sort.by(asc("beerName")) so paging is deterministic.
  • It picks one of four repository calls based on which of beerName / beerStyle are present (findAll otherwise).
  • showInventory=false nulls quantityOnHand on the loaded (detached) entities — response shaping only, never flushed.
  • Returns Page<BeerDTO> via beerPage.map(mapper).
  • Known edge: pageSize=0 or negative isn't clamped and would throw IllegalArgumentException → HTTP 500.

BeerServiceImpl/CustomerServiceImpl are HashMap-backed, seeded with 3 records, and exist to satisfy the same interface without a database.

Web layer

  • BeerController (/api/v1/beer) exposes GET (paged, returns Page<BeerDTO>), GET by id, POST (201 + Location), PUT, PATCH, DELETE. CustomerController mirrors it at /api/v1/customer but returns plain lists.
  • DTOs (BeerDTO, CustomerDTO) carry Bean Validation annotations (@NotBlank, @NotNull) and Jackson @JsonDeserialize(builder=...) for Lombok builders. There are no DTOs for orders or categories.
  • Errors: NotFoundException is @ResponseStatus(404); CustomErrorController (@ControllerAdvice) maps TransactionSystemException → 400 and MethodArgumentNotValidException → 400 with a field-error list.

Seeding

BootstrapData (a CommandLineRunner) runs on startup: seeds 3 hand-written beers if empty, loads csvdata/beers.csv (~2410 rows) when the beer count is under 10 (mapping CSV styles to the enum, price fixed at 10), and seeds 3 customers. That's why MariaDB holds 2429 beers.

Configuration / profiles

  • Default profile: H2 in-memory, Flyway disabled, Docker Compose disabled.
  • localmysql profile: MariaDB at 127.0.0.1:3306/restdb (user restadmin), Flyway enabled, ddl-auto=validate, a Hikari pool named RestDB-Pool (size 5, prepared-statement caching), and SQL/format/bind logging on. Note I locally set spring.docker.compose.enabled=false here because Docker isn't available — that's the one uncommitted change in the tree.

Tests

  • @DataJpaTest slices: BootstrapDataTest (asserts 2413 beers / 3 customers after seeding), CustomerRepositoryTest, BeerRepositoryTest (name search).
  • @SpringBootTest: BeerControllerIT (16 tests covering the full query/paging matrix), CustomerControllerIT, Spring7RestMvcApplicationTests, and the print-style BeerOrderRepositoryTest.
  • @WebMvcTest + Mockito: BeerControllerTest, CustomerControllerTest — these fail in this environment due to the JDK 25 Mockito/ByteBuddy self-attach limitation, not a code problem.
  • MySqlIT: Testcontainers MySQL 9.2 with @ServiceConnection and @ActiveProfiles("localmysql") — requires Docker, so it won't run here.
  • BeerCsvServiceImplTest: plain unit test of CSV parsing.

Cross-cutting observations, risks, and gaps

  • No order/category API yet. There are no services, DTOs, mappers, or controllers for BeerOrder/BeerOrderLine/Category, so all that model work is unreachable over HTTP — the next branches are expected to build the order service/API.
  • Bidirectional M2M with both sides owning the same join table (no mappedBy) — works for reads/validation, but risks duplicate join-row inserts when persisting both directions; likely revisited in branch 106.
  • Type drift: Integer+LocalDateTime (Beer/Customer) vs Long+java.sql.Timestamp (order/category entities); Category also uses the deprecated @GenericGenerator (compile warnings) instead of @UuidGenerator.
  • Uninitialized collections on Beer.categories, Beer.beerOrderLines, BeerOrder.beerOrderLines (only Customer.beerOrders is initialized) — they can be null.
  • BeerOrder.setCustomer has no null guard and the builder always routes through it, so an order can't be built without a non-null customer; and Category.equals/hashCode keyed on a mutable description is unsafe for Set membership.
  • No cascade / orphanRemoval, so order lines won't persist automatically with an order.
  • Default fetch types (@ManyToOne EAGER, @OneToMany LAZY) plus two eager associations on BeerOrderLine invite N+1 queries.
  • Schema nits: V4's drop order is reversed vs V3 (drops the referenced category before beer_category); category.created_date is timestamp while last_modified_date is datetime(6); and description varchar(50) vs Hibernate's implicit 255 (lengths aren't validated).
  • Hibernate logs open-in-view is enabled and a "dialect need not be specified" warning — both informational.

The app (PID 30299) and MariaDB are still running from the branch-105 run. Want me to stop them?
